    - **G5.** A mod cannot import `scripts/hooks/profile-path-scan.mjs`: `validate` refuses any import from outside the plugin folder, and a mod has no Node. The scanner can run through `$.process.run`, which takes `stdin`, but the scanner reads content only from a file. So G5 needs a scanner stdin mode (a repository-script change), a scratch-file write by the mod, or to leave v0.
    - **N1.** No `turn.step` hook is needed: `$.session.usage()` types `context.percent` and `context.window`. The line goes through the typed `context` field, which the model reads and the user never sees.

    - **Limits** are confirmed (10 s, and 1 s for the `.catch`). A hook that throws or overruns with no denying `.catch` fails open, and so does a `.catch` that returns nothing. The clock stops during `$` calls, so each `$.process.run` needs its own short `timeoutMs`.
    - **Packaging.** `hooks.json` takes `"modules": ["./register.js"]` (an array of exactly one path). A marketplace manifest at `tools/mods/.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` validates.
